HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B002","root_000262/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0314c7614251ed742fc5","text":"3. **Nearness preserved by distance.** Evidence: `ج ن ب` B002 (nearness/companionship at side) and B003 (distance/foreignness), activated by 92:20 “face of his Lord.” Structural cue: being distanced from fire prepares proximity to the Lord. Mechanism: one must be made foreign to the blaze to be companioned toward the divine face. Abductive move: I infer a two-sided separation: away from harm, toward presence. Reading change: تجنيب is not exile; it is protective repositioning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_636b4d78802e4f414469","text":"1. **Being kept to the side as protection.** Evidence: `ج ن ب` B003 (avoidance/removal to the side), `و ق ي` B002 (self-protective piety). Structural cue: it reverses 92:15’s contact with fire. Mechanism: the most guarded one is made lateral to the blaze, not exposed to it. Abductive move: I infer that prior self-guarding is answered by divine side-placement. Reading change: salvation is spatial: a merciful removal from the line of contact.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_699a377dcbbcb8d07ae2","text":"92:10’s identical facilitation verb was the strongest correction: ease is not simply comfort but being readied for a destination, good or terrible. 92:19 also weakened any “easy share” reading; true yusr lies outside repayable allotment and gambling-like accounting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6ab4b07c7e07cab8512e","text":"92:18 made the superlative’s internal guarding concrete as giving and self-purification. 92:19–20 then added a second kind of barrier—exclusion of repayment and redirection of motive—so the later conduct protects not only from the fire but also from a human-credit logic.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B001","root_000262/B003","root_000262/B011","root_001677/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_780e654e247678f7831e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:17:1\",\"root_000262\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B011\"]],[\"92:17:2\",\"root_001677\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Güvenlik, ateşin söndürülmesiyle değil, kişinin onun erişimi dışındaki bir yana alınması ve araya koruyucu bir sınır konmasıyla sağlanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8aaf21d2db3d14d17add","text":"1. **External removal meets internal guarding.** `يُجَنَّبُهَا` anchors `ج ن ب:B003` (keeping aside, avoiding, and removing from harm), while `ٱلْأَتْقَى` anchors `و ق ي:B002` (placing the self in protective caution) over `و ق ي:B001` (a protective barrier). The passive future supplies an external act of removal, whereas the superlative names an agent characterized by self-guarding; giving and purification in 92:18 then give that inward posture material conduct. The packet supplies the two layers but not their causal fit. The abductive reading is that practiced guarding and granted separation interlock, so avoidance of the fire is neither wholly self-produced nor unrelated to the agent’s formed posture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b852cd67e57544905faa","text":"2. **Protection as a changed spatial relation.** `ج ن ب:B001` supplies side/edge geometry, `ج ن ب:B003` supplies removal to that side, and `ج ن ب:B011` supplies a shield held at one’s side. Against the blazing fire of 92:14–15, the verb describes danger management by distance and interposed protection rather than by extinguishing the danger. The shield image is analogical, and the packet does not state what mediates the removal. The reading change is spatially precise: safety consists in being placed outside the fire’s reach, with a guarded boundary between subject and threat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B002","root_000262/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b916b3e29dec77598621","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:17:1\",\"root_000262\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ateşe yabancılaştıran uzaklık, aynı zamanda kişiyi Rabbin yüzüne yakınlığa hazırlayan iki yönlü bir konum değişikliği olarak okunabilir; ikinci yön dolaylıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B003","root_001677/B001","root_001677/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d8f172f4434937cc6080","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:17:1\",\"root_000262\",[\"B003\"]],[\"92:17:2\",\"root_001677\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kişinin içten koruyucu sakınması ile dışarıdan ateşin yanına düşmeyecek biçimde uzaklaştırılması birbirini tamamlayan iki koruma katmanı olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f44bdc2b8ed25defba4e","text":"2. **Side-shield imagery.** Evidence: `ج ن ب` B011 (shield/cover at the side), `و ق ي` B001 (protective barrier). Structural cue: the fire warning makes protection materially relevant. Mechanism: taqwā becomes a side-shield, and divine action completes it by moving the person away. Abductive move: I infer that moral guarding is embodied as shielding geometry. Reading change: الأتقى is not merely morally better; he is shield-configured.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
